                                               controlling or controlled by or under direct or indirect common control with such
                                               specified person

                                               制或與該特定人士直接或間接受共同控制的任何其他人士

                                               May 18, 2026

“Company” or “our Company”                Shanghai REFIRE Group Limited (上海重塑能源集團股份有限公司), a joint stock
                                          company with limited liability incorporated in the PRC, the predecessor of which
                                          was Shanghai REFIRE Group Ltd. (上海重塑能源集團有限公司) (formerly known as
                                          Hangzhou REFIRE Technology Co., Ltd. (重塑能源科技(杭州)有限公司)), a limited
                                          liability company established in the PRC on September 18, 2015, and if the context
                                          requires, includes its predecessor

                                          年9月18日在中國成立的有限責任公司，如文意所指，包括其前身


“Components for Fuel Cell System”         components self-developed and produced by our Group, including fuel cell stacks,
                                          MEAs, bipolar plates, hydrogen circulation systems, DC-to-DC boost converters, etc.
「燃料電池系統零部件」                           指   本集團自主研發生產的零部件，包括燃料電池電堆、膜電極、雙極板、氫循環系
                                          統、直流升壓轉換器等


“Components for Fuel Cell Vehicles”       components sold to our customers for producing fuel cell vehicles, including hydrogen
                                          supply systems, fuel cell engine accessories, energy storage systems, etc.
「燃料電池汽車零部件」                           指   向客戶銷售的用於生產燃料電池汽車的零部件，包括供氫系統、燃料電池發動機
                                          配件、儲能系統等

energy integration. We partnered with China State Shipbuilding Corporation        索氫能船舶標準化、規模化應用，加速推動航
Limited (CSSC) to co-develop marine fuel cell systems, and collaborated           運綠色轉型；參與編製的《工業副產氫碳排放核

hydrogen-powered vessels, accelerating green transformation in inland             精準核算工業副產氫的碳排放提供清晰統一的

Accounting of Industrial By-product Hydrogen” that we participated in

the RMB and other currencies relating to the Group’s business. Further, the     波動所產生的外匯風險。此外，本集團面臨的
Group is subject to foreign currency risk attributable to the bank balances     外匯風險源自以人民幣以外的貨幣計值的銀行
that are denominated in currencies other than RMB. The Group seeks to limit     結餘。本集團尋求通過盡量降低外幣淨頭寸來
the exposure to foreign currency risk by minimizing its net foreign currency    限制所面臨的外匯風險。截至 2025 年12 月31
position. The Group did not enter into any hedging transactions in respect of   日，本集團並無就外匯風險訂立任何對沖交
foreign currency risk as of December 31, 2025. The Directors expect that the    易。董事預期人民幣匯率的波動將不會對本集

on the operation of the Group.

chain in the field of fuel cells and hydrogen production systems, we provide   需求提供製氫及終端應用一站式解決方案，推

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                         股息政策
As the Company intends to retain all of its future earnings to finance the              由於本公司擬保留所有未來利潤以為本公司業
development and growth of the Company’s business, it has not yet adopted a              務的發展及增長提供資金，其尚未採納宣派或
dividend policy to declare or pay any dividends. The declaration and payment of         派付任何股息的股息政策。日後任何股息宣派
any dividends in the future will be determined by our Board and subject to the          及派付將由董事會釐定並須遵守公司章程及《中
Articles of Association and the Company Law of the People’s Republic of China           華人民共和國公司法》，且將取決於多種因素，
(《中華人民共和國公司法》), and will depend on a number of factors, including                       包括我們的財務表現、業務經營、資本要求及
our financial performance and business operation, capital requirements and              合約限制。股息僅可自可用於合法分派的利潤
contractual restrictions. No dividend shall be declared or payable except out of        及儲備中宣派或派付。誠如中國法律顧問確
our profits and reserves lawfully available for distribution. As confirmed by our       認，根據中國法律，本公司日後獲得的任何利
PRC legal adviser, according to the PRC laws, any future net profits obtained by        潤淨額必須首先用於彌補其累計過往虧損，其
the Company must first be used to make up for its accumulated prior losses,             後本公司須將其利潤淨額的至少10%分配至法定
after which the Company is obliged to allocate at least 10% of its net profits to       公積金，直至法定公積金超過本公司註冊資本
the statutory capital reserve until the cumulative amount of the statutory capital      50%為止。因此，本公司將僅能於(i)已經彌補其
reserve exceeds 50% of the Company’ registered capital. Therefore, the Company          過往財政年度的全部過往累計虧損；及(ii)本公
will only be able to declare dividends after (i) all its historical accumulated         司已分配充足利潤淨額至上述法定公積金，方
losses from prior fiscal years have been made up for; and (ii) the Company has          能宣派股息。董事會將定期審閱本公司狀況並
allocated sufficient net profits to the statutory capital reserve as described above.   考慮於適當時候採納股息政策。
The Board will regularly review the Company’ status and consider adopting a
dividend policy if and when appropriate.

2025 H SHARE INCENTIVE SCHEME                                                    2025年H股激勵計劃
(a) Adoption Date and Duration of the Scheme                                     (a) 採納日期及計劃期限
   The Company has adopted the 2025 H Share Incentive Scheme at the                本公司於2025年11月6日舉行的股東會上採
   shareholders’ general meeting held on November 6, 2025. The 2025 H              納2025年H股激勵計劃。2025年H股激勵計
   Share Incentive Scheme shall be valid and effective for the period of           劃自 2025 年 11 月 6 日起計十年內有效及生
   ten years commencing on the November 6, 2025. As at the date of this            效，於本年報日期，其剩餘期限約為九年
   annual report, its remaining term was approximately nine years and six          六個月。
   months.


(b) Purposes of the Scheme                                                       (b) 計劃目的
   The purposes of the 2025 H Share Incentive Scheme are: (i) to attract,          2025年H股激勵計劃旨在：(i)通過提供擁有
   motivate and retain skilled and experienced personnel to strive for the         本公司股權的機會，招攬、激勵及挽留技
   future development and expansion of the Group by providing them with            術熟練與經驗豐富的人員為本集團的未來
   the opportunity to own equity interests in the Company; (ii) to deepen          發展及擴張而努力；(ii)深化本公司薪酬制度
   the reform on the Company’s remuneration system and to develop                  改革，發展及不斷完善股東、運營及執行
   and constantly improve the interests balance mechanism among the                管理人員之間的利益平衡機制；及(iii) (a)肯
   Shareholders, the operational and executive management; and (iii) to            定本公司領導層（執行及非執行董事）的貢
   (a) recognize the contributions of the leadership of the Company (the           獻；(b) 鼓勵、激勵及挽留對本集團持續經
   executive and non-executive Directors); (b) encourage, motivate and             營、發展及長期增長作出有利貢獻的本公
   retain the leadership of the Company whose contributions are beneficial         司領導層；及(c)為本公司領導層及長期僱
   to the continual operation, development and long-term growth of the             員提供其他獎勵使本公司領導層的利益與
   Group; and (c) provide additional incentive for the leadership of the           股東及本集團整體利益一致。
   Company and long standing employee by aligning the interests of the
   leadership of the Company to those of the Shareholders and the Group
   as a whole.

CHARITABLE DONATIONS                                                           慈善捐贈
The Group donated HK$500,000 to aid victims of the Hong Kong Tai Po            截至2025 年12 月31 日止年度，本集團向香港大
fire, supporting emergency rescue, humanitarian relief, and post-disaster      埔火災捐款港幣50 萬元，支援受災群眾緊急救

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                             風險管理及內部控制
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management and             董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制系統負有
internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness. Such systems          責任，並負責檢討其成效。該等系統旨在管理
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve       而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，且僅能就
business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not absolute             並無重大失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對保
assurance against material misstatement or loss.                                  證。


The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and determining the       董事會全面負責評估及釐定本公司達成戰略目
nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving the Company’s   標時所願意承擔的風險性質及程度，並設立及
strategic objectives, and establishing and maintaining appropriate and            維護適當而有效的風險管理及內部控制系統。
effective risk management and internal control systems.

   2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                      2.1 編製基準
       These financial statements have been prepared in accordance with            該等財務報表根據國際會計準則理事會
       IFRS Accounting Standards, (which include International Accounting         （「國際會計準則理事會」）發佈的國際財務
       Standards (“IASs”) and interpretations) issued by the International         報告準則會計準則（包括國際會計準則（「國
       Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”) and the disclosure                  際會計準則」）及詮釋）及香港公司條例披露
       requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance. They have                規定編製。該等財務報表乃根據歷史成本
       been prepared under the historical cost convention, except for              法編製，惟按公允價值計量的私募股權基
       private equity funds and equity investments which have been                 金及股權投資除外。該等財務報表乃以人
       measured at fair value. These financial statements are presented            民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，除另有指明外，所
       in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the nearest               有數值均四捨五入至最接近的千位數。
       thousand except when otherwise indicated.


       Basis of consolidation                                                      綜合基準
       The consolidated financial statements include the financial                 綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司（統
       statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively                稱為「本集團」）截至2025年12月31日止年度
       referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2025.            的財務報表。附屬公司為本公司直接或間
       A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly or      接控制的實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集
       indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when             團對參與投資對象業務的可變回報承擔風
       the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its           險或享有權利，且有能力運用其對投資對
       involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect those           象的權力（即本集團獲賦予現有能力以主導
       returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights that     投資對象相關業務的既存權利）影響該等回
       give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant activities of     報時，即取得控制權。
       the investee).


       Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights          一般情況下，存在多數投票權形成控制權
       results in control. When the Company has less than a majority of            的假定。倘本公司擁有投資對象投票權或
       the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all        類似權利不過半數，本集團評估是否對投
       relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether it has power          資對象擁有權力時，會考慮所有相關事實
       over an investee, including:                                                及情況，包括：


       (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the          (a) 與投資對象其他投票持有人的合約安
           investee;                                                                 排；


       (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                 (b) 其他合約安排所產生的權利；及


       (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                  (c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

     be consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                   日為止。


     Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income            損益及其他全面收益的各組成部分歸屬於
     are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group and to the         本集團母公司擁有人及非控股權益，儘管
     non-controlling interests, even if this results in the non-controlling     這會導致非控股權益結餘出現虧絀。有關
     interests having a deficit balance. All intra-group assets and             本集團成員公司間交易的所有集團內公司

     transactions between members of the Group are eliminated in full           流量於綜合入賬時悉數對銷。
     on consolidation.


     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts       倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制因素
     and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more           中，有一項或多項出現變數，本集團會重
     of the three elements of control described above. A change in              新評估其是否控制投資對象。於附屬公司
     the ownership interest of a subsidiary, without a loss of control, is      的所有權權益變動（並無失去控制權）按權
     accounted for as an equity transaction.                                    益交易入賬。


     If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it derecognises the          倘本集團失去對一家附屬公司的控制權，
     related assets (including goodwill), liabilities, any non-controlling      其 會 終 止 確 認 相 關 資 產（ 包 括 商 譽 ）、 負
     interest and the exchange fluctuation reserve; and recognises the          債、任何非控股權益及匯率波動儲備，並
     fair value of any investment retained and any resulting surplus or         於損益中確認所保留任何投資的公允價值
     deficit in profit or loss. The Group’s share of components previously      及任何因此產生的盈餘或虧絀。先前於其
     recognised in other comprehensive income is reclassified to profit         他全面收益中確認的本集團應佔部分按猶
     or loss or retained profits, as appropriate, on the same basis as          如本集團直接出售有關資產或負債時被要
     would be required if the Group had directly disposed of the related        求的相同基準重新分類至損益或留存收益
     assets or liabilities.                                                    （如適用）。

2. ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                            2. 會計政策（續）
  2.3 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                             2.3 重大會計政策
     Investments in associates and a joint venture                                於聯營公司及一家合營企業的投
                                                                                  資
     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long-term interest        聯營公司指本集團擁有一般不少於20%
     of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights and over          的投票權的長期權益且對其具有重大
     which it has significant influence. Significant influence is the power       影響的實體。重大影響是指參與投資
     to participate in the financial and operating policy decisions of the        對象的財務及經營政策決策的權力，
     investee, but is not control or joint control over those policies.           但並非對該等政策的控制或共同控制。


     A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties           合營企業指一種合營安排，對安排擁
     that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the                有共同控制權的訂約方據此對合營企
     net assets of the joint ventures. Joint control is the contractually         業的資產淨值擁有權利。共同控制是
     agreed sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only               指按照相關約定對某項安排所共有的
     when decisions about the relevant activities require the unanimous           控制，並且該安排的相關活動必須經
     consent of the parties sharing control.                                      過分享控制權的參與方一致同意後才
                                                                                  能決策。


     The Group’s investments in associates and a joint venture are stated         本集團於聯營公司及一家合營企業的
     in the consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s           投資乃根據權益會計法按本集團所佔
     share of net assets under the equity method of accounting, less              資產淨值減任何減值損失計入綜合財
     any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into line               務狀況表。對於可能存在的任何不同
     any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist. The Group’s share         會計政策，均會作出調整使其一致。
     of the post-acquisition results and other comprehensive income               本集團應佔聯營公司和合營企業的收
     of associates and joint ventures is included in the consolidated             購後業績和其他全面收益的份額分別
     statement of profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive             計入綜合損益表及綜合其他全面收益
     income, respectively. In addition, when there has been a change              表。此外，倘直接於聯營公司或合營
     recognised directly in the equity of the associate or joint ventures,        企業的權益確認變動，則本集團於綜
     the Group recognises its share of any changes, when applicable,              合權益變動表確認其任何變動的份額
     in the consolidated statement of changes in equity. Unrealised              （如適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合
     gains and losses resulting from transactions between the Group               營企業之間因交易產生的未變現收益
     and its associates or joint ventures are eliminated to the extent            或虧損，在本集團於聯營公司或合營
     of the Group’s investments in the associates or joint ventures,              企業中的投資的範圍內予以抵銷，但
     except where unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment             有證據表明未變現虧損屬於所轉讓資
     of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition             產發生減值的情況除外。本集團對聯
     of associates or joint ventures is included as part of the Group’s           營公司或合營企業的投資包含因收購
     investments in associates or joint ventures.                                 聯營公司或合營企業所產生的商譽。

       Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition               業務合併採用購買法入賬。轉讓的代
       method. The consideration transferred is measured at the                    價乃按收購日期的公允價值計量，該

       acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects whether           的總和。就每項業務合併而言，本集
       to measure the non-controlling interests in the acquiree at fair            團選擇是否以公允價值或按被收購方
       value or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable          的可辨別資產淨值的應佔比例，計量
       net assets. All other components of non-controlling interests are           於被收購方的非控股權益。非控股權
       measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are expensed as           益的所有其他組成部分按公允價值計
       incurred.                                                                   量。收購相關成本於產生時支銷。


       The Group determines that it has acquired a business when                   當所收購的一組活動及資產包括一項
       the acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and a           資源投入及一項實質過程，而兩者對
       substantive process that together significantly contribute to the           創造產出的能力有重大貢獻，本集團
       ability to create outputs.                                                  認為其已收購一項業務。

       in accordance with the contractual terms, economic circumstances            條件，評估所承擔的金融資產及負
       and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes          債，以作出適合的分類及指定，其中
       the separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the             包括分開被收購方主合約中的嵌入式
       acquiree.                                                                   衍生工具。

       Fair value measurement                                                       公允價值計量
       The Group measures its equity investments and private equity                 本集團於各報告期末以公允價值計量
       funds at fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is       其股權投資及私募股權基金。公允價
       the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer        值是指市場參與者於計量日發生的有
       a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants at         序交易中，出售一項資產所能收到或
       the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on                 轉移一項負債所需支付的價格。公允
       the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer           價值計量假定出售資產或轉讓負債的
       the liability takes place either in the principal market for the asset       交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在無主
       or liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most           要市場情況下）最有利市場中進行。主
       advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal or the         要市場或最有利市場必須是本集團能
       most advantageous market must be accessible by the Group. The                夠進入的市場。計量資產和負債公允
       fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using the assumptions      價值時，採用市場參與者對該資產或
       that market participants would use when pricing the asset or                 負債定價時為實現其經濟利益最大化
       liability, assuming that market participants act in their economic           所使用的假設。
       best interest.


       A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account         非金融資產的公允價值計量考慮市場
       a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by              參與者將該資產用於最佳用途產生經
       using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another      濟利益的能力，或者將該資產出售給
       market participant that would use the asset in its highest and best          能夠用於最佳用途的其他市場參與者
       use.                                                                         產生經濟利益的能力。

     Fair value measurement (continued)                                                        公允價值計量（續）
     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                           本集團採用在有關情況下適用並且有
     circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure                      足夠數據計量公允價值的估值技術，
     fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and                          並盡可能多地使用相關可觀察輸入
     minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                                值，盡可能少地使用不可觀察輸入值。


     All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or disclosed                  所有其公允價值在財務報表中計量或
     in the financial statements are categorised within the fair value                         披露的資產和負債，均根據對公允價
     hierarchy, described as follows, based on the lowest level input that                     值計量整體具有重大意義的最低層級
     is significant to the fair value measurement as a whole:                                  的輸入值，在下述公允價值層級內分
                                                                                               類：


     Level 1 – based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets                           第一層級 － 根據同類資產或負債在活
                   for identical assets or liabilities                                                      躍市場上的報價（未經調
                                                                                                            整）
     Level 2 – b a s e d o n v a l u a t i o n t e c h n i q u e s f o r w h i c h t h e       第二層級 － 根據對公允價值計量具有
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair value                                 重大意義的直接或間接可
                   measurement is observable, either directly or indirectly                                 觀察最低層級的輸入值的
                                                                                                            估值方法
     Level 3 – b a s e d o n v a l u a t i o n t e c h n i q u e s f o r w h i c h t h e       第三層級 － 根據對公允價值計量具有
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair value                                 重大意義的不可觀察最低
                   measurement is unobservable                                                              層級的輸入值的估值方法


     For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial                           對於持續在財務報表確認的資產和負
     statements on a recurring basis, the Group determines whether                             債，本集團在各報告期末通過重新評
     transfers have occurred between levels in the hierarchy by                                估分類（根據對整體公允價值計量具有
     reassessing categorisation (based on the lowest level input that is                       重大意義的最低層級的輸入值）確定各
     significant to the fair value measurement as a whole) at the end of                       層級之間是否存在轉移。
     each reporting period.

       Impairment of non-financial assets                                           非金融資產減值
       Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual                     若存在任何減值跡象，或對一項資產
       impairment testing for an asset is required (other than inventories,         （存貨、合約資產、遞延稅項資產及金
       contract assets, deferred tax assets and financial assets), the asset’s      融資產除外）進行年度減值測試，則估
       recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount               計該項資產的可收回金額。一項資產
       is the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value in use          的可收回金額根據資產或現金產生單
       and its fair value less costs of disposal, and is determined for an          位的使用價值與其公允價值減出售成
       individual asset, unless the asset does not generate cash inflows            本兩者中的較高者計算，並以個別資
       that are largely independent of those from other assets or groups of         產釐定，但如果該項資產並不產生基
       assets, in which case the recoverable amount is determined for the           本獨立於其他資產或資產組別的現金
       cash-generating unit to which the asset belongs.                             流入，則可收回金額則按該項資產所
                                                                                    屬的現金產生單位釐定。


       In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, a portion of the           對現金產生單位進行減值測試時，倘
       carrying amount of a corporate asset (e.g., a headquarters building)         可以合理及一致基準進行分配，公司
       is allocated to an individual cash-generating unit if it can be              資產（如總部大樓）賬面值的一部分分
       allocated on a reasonable and consistent basis or, otherwise, to the         配至個別現金產生單位或最小現金產
       smallest group of cash-generating units.                                     生單位組別。


       An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount of              僅在資產賬面值高於其可收回金額的
       an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in use,          情況下，方會確認減值損失。評估使
       the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present              用價值時，採用反映目前市場對貨幣
       value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market             時間價值及資產特定風險的評估的稅
       assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific to             前折現率將估計未來現金流量折現至
       the asset. An impairment loss is charged to the statement of profit          現值。減值損失於產生期間計入損益
       or loss in the period in which it arises in those expense categories         表，並根據相關減值資產的功能確定
       consistent with the function of the impaired asset.                          費用相關分類。


       An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as                 於各報告期末，評估是否有跡象顯示
       to whether there is an indication that previously recognised                 先前已確認的減值損失不再存在或可
       impairment losses may no longer exist or may have decreased. If              能減少。若出現上述跡象，則估計可
       such an indication exists, the recoverable amount is estimated.              收回金額。在用以釐定資產可收回金
       A previously recognised impairment loss of an asset other than               額的估計值發生變化時，方會撥回先
       goodwill is reversed only if there has been a change in the                  前確認的資產（商譽除外）減值損失，
       estimates used to determine the recoverable amount of that asset,            惟撥回的金額不可高於倘以往年度並
       but not to an amount higher than the carrying amount that would              無就資產確認減值損失而應釐定的賬
       have been determined (net of any depreciation/amortisation) had              面值（扣除任何折舊╱攤銷）。減值損
       no impairment loss been recognised for the asset in prior years. A           失撥回於產生期間計入損益表。
       reversal of such an impairment loss is credited to the statement of
       profit or loss in the period in which it arises.

       Property, plant and equipment and depreciation                               物業、廠房及設備以及折舊
       Property, plant and equipment, other than construction in                    物 業 、 廠 房 及 設 備（ 在 建 工 程 除 外 ）
       progress, are stated at cost less accumulated depreciation and               按成本減累計折舊及任何減值損失列
       any impairment losses. The cost of an item of property, plant                賬。物業、廠房及設備項目的成本包
       and equipment comprises its purchase price and any directly                  括其購買價及使該項資產用於其預定

       location for its intended use.                                               直接應佔成本。

       have been put into operation, such as repairs and maintenance, is            生的支出（如維修保養費）一般於其產
       normally charged to the statement of profit or loss in the period in         生期間自損益表扣除。倘達到確認標
       which it is incurred. In situations where the recognition criteria are       準，則重大檢查的開支作為重置成本
       satisfied, the expenditure for a major inspection is capitalised in the      於資產賬面值中資本化。倘須定期更
       carrying amount of the asset as a replacement. Where significant             換物業、廠房及設備的重要部分，則
       parts of property, plant and equipment are required to be replaced           本集團會將該等部分作為有特定可使
       at intervals, the Group recognises such parts as individual assets           用年期的個別資產確認並相應計提折
       with specific useful lives and depreciates them accordingly.                 舊。


       Depreciation is calculated on the straight-line basis to write off the       折舊採用直線法計算，於各項物業、
       cost of each item of property, plant and equipment to its residual           廠房及設備的估計可使用年期內將其
       value over its estimated useful life. The principal annual rates used        成本撇銷至其剩餘價值。就此所採用

                                                            terms and 16%                                  為準）

       Where parts of an item of property, plant and equipment have                 倘物業、廠房及設備項目各部分有不
       different useful lives, the cost of that item is allocated on a              同的可使用年期，該項目的成本將按
       reasonable basis among the parts and each part is depreciated                合理基準在不同部分中作出分配，而

         option). It also applies the recognition exemption for leases                 團亦對被視為低價值的辦公設備租
         of low-value assets to leases of office equipment that is                     賃應用低價值資產租賃確認豁免。
         considered to be of low value.


         Lease payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value                   短期租賃和低價值資產租賃的租賃

         over the lease term.                                                          費用。

     Initial recognition and measurement                                          初始確認及計量
     Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as                  於初始確認時，金融資產分類為其後
     subsequently measured at amortised cost, fair value through other            按攤銷成本、按公允價值計入其他全
     comprehensive income, and fair value through profit or loss.                 面收益及按公允價值計入損益計量。


     The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends        初始確認金融資產分類取決於金融資
     on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow characteristics and the       產的合約現金流量特徵，以及本集團
     Group’s business model for managing them. With the exception                 管理金融資產的業務模式。除並未包
     of trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing             含重大融資組成部分的貿易應收款項
     component or for which the Group has applied the practical                   或本集團已實行實際權宜辦法不作調
     expedient of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing             整重大融資組成部分影響的貿易應收
     component, the Group initially measures a financial asset at its fair        款項外，本集團按其公允價值初步計
     value plus in the case of a financial asset not at fair value through        量金融資產，倘金融資產並非按公允
     profit or loss, transaction costs. Trade receivables that do not             價值計入損益計量，則另加交易成
     contain a significant financing component or for which the Group             本。並未包含重大融資組成部分的貿
     has applied the practical expedient are measured at the transaction          易應收款項或本集團已實行實際權宜
     price determined under IFRS 15 in accordance with the policies set           辦法的貿易應收款項乃按照下文「收入
     out for “Revenue recognition” below.                                         確認」所載政策根據國際財務報告準則

2. ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                               2. 會計政策（續）
   2.3 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                   2.3 重大會計政策（續）
       Impairment of financial assets                                               金融資產減值
       The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                 本集團確認所有並非持作按公允價值
       (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through             計入損益的債務工具的預期信貸虧損
       profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the                 （「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧
       contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all           損是基於根據合約到期的合約現金流
       the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an           量與本集團預期收到的所有現金流量
       approximation of the original effective interest rate. The expected          之間的差額，以原實際利率的近似值
       cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of collateral held          進行折現。預期現金流量將包括出售
       or other credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual            所持抵押品的現金流量或合約條款所
       terms.                                                                       包含的其他信貸升級措施的現金流量。


       General approach                                                             一般方法
       ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which            預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。對於
       there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial       自初始確認以來信用風險沒有大幅增
       recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from            加的信用風險敞口，對未來 12 個月內
       default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a                可能發生的違約事件（12個月預期信貸
       12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has been           虧損）造成的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧
       a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss      損撥備。對於自初始確認以來信用風
       allowance is required for credit losses expected over the remaining          險大幅增加的信用風險敞口，無論違
       life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a           約的時間長短（全期預期信貸虧損），
       lifetime ECL).                                                               都需要對風險敞口剩餘年期內預期信
                                                                                    貸虧損計提虧損撥備。


       At the end of the reporting period, the Group assesses whether               於報告期末，本集團評估金融工具的
       the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly        信用風險自初始確認以來是否大幅增
       since initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group             加。在進行評估時，本集團將報告日
       compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument         期金融工具發生違約的風險與初始確
       as at the reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on             認日期金融工具發生違約的風險進行
       the financial instrument as at the date of initial recognition               比較，並考慮無需付出過多成本或努
       and considers reasonable and supportable information that is                 力即可取得的合理有理據資料，包括
       available without undue cost or effort, including historical and             歷史及前瞻性資料。
       forward-looking information.

2. ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                           2. 會計政策（續）
  2.3 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                2.3 重大會計政策（續）
     Impairment of financial assets (continued)                                  金融資產減值（續）
     General approach (continued)                                                一般方法（續）
     The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual           當合約付款逾期360天時，本集團將金
     payments are 360 days past due. However, in certain cases, the              融資產視為違約。然而，於若干情況
     Group may also consider a financial asset to be in default when             下，當內部或外部資料表明本集團在
     internal or external information indicates that the Group is unlikely       考慮其擁有的任何信貸升級措施之前
     to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking        不太可能收到全部未付合約金額時，
     into account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                     本集團亦會將金融資產視為違約。


     A financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable                倘無合理預期收回合約現金流量，則
     expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.                       撇銷金融資產。


     Debt investments at fair value through other comprehensive                  按公允價值計入其他全面收益的債權
     income and financial assets at amortised cost are subject to                投資及按攤銷成本計量的金融資產，
     impairment under the general approach and they are classified               按照一般方法進行減值，並在以下階
     within the following stages for measurement of ECLs except for              段對其進行分類，以計量預期信貸虧
     trade receivables and contract assets which apply the simplified            損，但採用簡化方法的貿易應收款項
     approach as detailed below.                                                 及合約資產除外，具體如下。


     Stage 1 – Financial instruments for which credit risk has not               第一階段 – 信用風險自初始確認以來
                 increased significantly since initial recognition and for                    沒有大幅增加的金融工
                 which the loss allowance is measured at an amount                            具，虧損撥備的計量金額
                 equal to 12-month ECLs                                                       等於12個月預期信貸虧損
     Stage 2 – Financial instruments for which credit risk has increased         第二階段 – 信用風險自初始確認以來
                 significantly since initial recognition but that are not                     大幅增加，但並非已發生
                 credit-impaired financial assets and for which the loss                      信貸減值金融資產的金融
                 allowance is measured at an amount equal to lifetime                         工具，虧損撥備的計量金
                 ECLs                                                                         額等於全期預期信貸虧損
     Stage 3 – Financial assets that are credit-impaired at the reporting        第三階段 – 於報告日期已發生信貸減
                 date (but that are not purchased or originated credit-                       值（但未購買或原已發生
                 impaired) and for which the loss allowance is measured                       信貸減值）的金融資產，
                 at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs                                          虧損撥備的計量金額等於
                                                                                              全期預期信貸虧損

       Simplified approach                                                             簡化方法
       For trade receivables and contract assets that do not contain a                 對於不包含重大融資組成部分的貿易
       significant financing component or when the Group applies the                   應收款項及合約資產，或當本集團採
       practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a significant                用不調整重大融資組成部分影響的實
       financing component, the Group applies the simplified approach                  際權宜方法時，本集團在計算預期信
       in calculating ECLs. Under the simplified approach, the Group                   貸虧損時採用簡化方法。根據簡化方
       does not track changes in credit risk, but instead recognises a loss            法，本集團不跟蹤信用風險的變化，
       allowance based on lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group              而是根據於各報告日期的全期預期信
       has established a provision matrix that is based on its historical              貸虧損確認虧損撥備。本集團已根據
       credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors specific           其歷史信貸虧損經驗建立了撥備矩
       to the debtors and the economic environment.                                    陣，並對債務人和經濟環境的特定前
                                                                                       瞻性因素進行了調整。


       For trade receivables and contract assets that contain a significant            對於包含重大融資組成部分的貿易應
       financing component and lease receivables, the Group chooses as                 收款項及合約資產以及租賃應收款
       its accounting policy to adopt the simplified approach in calculating           項，本集團選擇採用簡化方法計算預
       ECLs with policies as described above.                                          期信貸虧損，並採用上述政策作為其

     A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or                 若由於過往事項產生現有法定或推斷

     settle the obligation. The increase in the discounted present value           的折現現值增加計入損益表的財務費
     amount arising from the passage of time is included in finance                用。
     costs in the statement of profit or loss.

       countries in which the Group operates.

     Deferred tax assets are recognised for all deductible temporary               所有可抵扣暫時差額、結轉未動用稅
     differences, and the carryforward of unused tax credits and any               項抵免及任何未動用稅項虧損均確認
     unused tax losses. Deferred tax assets are recognised to the extent           遞延稅項資產，但以很有可能有應課
     that it is probable that taxable profit will be available against which       稅利潤抵銷可抵扣暫時差額以及結轉
     the deductible temporary differences, and the carryforward of                 未動用稅項抵免及未動用稅項虧損為
     unused tax credits and unused tax losses can be utilised, except:             限，除非：


     •   when the deferred tax asset relating to the deductible                    •     與可抵扣暫時差額相關的遞延稅項
         temporary differences arises from the initial recognition of                    資產是由初始確認非業務合併交易
         an asset or liability in a transaction that is not a business                   的資產或負債所產生，而在交易時
         combination and, at the time of the transaction, affects                        既不影響會計利潤亦不影響應課稅
         neither the accounting profit nor taxable profit or loss and                    利潤或虧損，且不產生相等的應課
         does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary                    稅及可抵扣暫時差額；及
         differences; and


     •   in respect of deductible temporary differences associated with            •     可抵扣暫時差額與於附屬公司、聯
         investments in subsidiaries, associates and a joint venture,                    營公司及合營企業的投資相關，則
         deferred tax assets are only recognised to the extent that it                   僅在可見未來很可能會撥回暫時差
         is probable that the temporary differences will reverse in the                  額，且有應課稅利潤可供抵銷以動
         foreseeable future and taxable profit will be available against                 用暫時差額的情況下，方會確認遞
         which the temporary differences can be utilised.                                延稅項資產。

  typically sell and charge prices to their customers after deducting           前，在扣除銷售時適用的補貼後，向其客

  Provision for impairment of trade receivables is made based on                 貿易應收款項減值撥備基於對貿易應收款

  Under the collective approach, the Group uses a provision matrix to            在組合法下，本集團使用撥備矩陣計算貿
  calculate ECLs for trade receivables. The provision rates are based            易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。撥備率基於
  on ageing analysis of customers that have similar loss patterns. The           具有類似虧損模式的客戶的賬齡分析計
  provision matrix is initially based on the Group’s historical observed         算。撥備矩陣最初基於本集團過往觀察得
  default rates. The Group will calibrate the matrix to adjust the historical    到的違約率確定。本集團將根據前瞻性資
  credit loss experience with forward-looking information. At each               料調整矩陣以調整過往信貸虧損經驗。於
  reporting date, the historical observed default rates are updated and          各報告日期更新過往觀察得到的違約率並
  changes in the forward-looking estimates are analysed.                         分析前瞻性估計的變動。


  The assessment of the correlation among historical observed default            對過往觀察得到的違約率、預測經濟狀況
  rates, forecast economic conditions and ECLs is a significant estimate.        與預期信貸虧損之間的相關性的評估乃重
  The amount of ECLs is sensitive to changes in circumstances and forecast       大估計。預期信貸虧損金額易受情況變動
  economic conditions. The Group’s historical credit loss experience and         及預測經濟狀況的影響。本集團的過往信
  forecast of economic conditions may also not be representative of a            貸虧損經驗及經濟狀況預測亦可能無法代
  customer’s actual default in the future. The information about the ECLs        表未來客戶的實際違約情況。有關本集團
  on the Group’s trade receivables is disclosed in note 21 to the financial      貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損資料於財務

   for all non-financial assets (including the right-of-use assets) at the          括使用權資產）是否存在任何減值跡象。當
   end of the reporting period. These non-financial assets are tested for           有跡象顯示非金融資產賬面值可能無法收
   impairment when there are indicators that the carrying amounts may               回時進行減值測試。當一項資產或現金產
   not be recoverable. Impairment exists when the carrying value of an              生單位的賬面值超過其可收回金額（即公允
   asset or a cash-generating unit exceeds its recoverable amount, which is         價值減出售成本與其使用價值兩者中的較

   disposing of the asset. When value-in-use calculations are undertaken,           時，管理層必須估計來自資產或現金產生
   management must estimate the expected future cash flows from the                 單位的預期未來現金流量，並選取合適的
   asset or cash-generating unit and choose a suitable discount rate in             折現率，以計算該等現金流量的現值。
   order to calculate the present value of those cash flows.

  Deferred tax assets                                                             遞延稅項資產
  Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses to the extent          倘很可能有應課稅利潤可動用虧損抵銷，
  that it is probable that taxable profit will be available against which the     則就未動用稅項虧損確認遞延稅項資產。
  losses can be utilised. Significant management judgement is required            管理層於釐定可予以確認的遞延稅項資產
  to determine the amount of deferred tax assets that can be recognised,          數額時，須根據可能出現未來應課稅利潤
  based upon the likely timing and the level of future taxable profits,           的時間及數額，以及未來稅務規劃策略作
  together with future tax planning strategies.                                   出重大判斷。


  The Group has tax losses of RMB3,338,476,000 (2024: RMB2,871,711,000)           本集團產生結轉稅項虧損人民幣3,338,476,000
  carried forward. These losses related to subsidiaries that have a history of    元（2024年：人民幣2,871,711,000元）。該等
  losses, have not expired, and may not be used to offset taxable income          虧損與過往產生虧損的附屬公司有關、未屆
  elsewhere in the Group. The subsidiaries have neither any taxable               滿且不得用於抵銷本集團的其他應課稅收
  temporary difference nor any tax planning opportunities available that          入。附屬公司既無任何應課稅暫時差額亦無
  could partly support the recognition of these losses as deferred tax            任何可用稅務規劃機會，可部分支持將該等
  assets. On this basis, the Group has determined that it cannot recognise        虧損確認為遞延稅項資產。根據該基準，本
  deferred tax assets on the tax losses carried forward. Further details are      集團確定其無法就結轉稅項虧損確認遞延稅
  contained in note 19 to the financial statements.                               項資產。進一步詳情載於財務報表附註19。

   Seasonality of operations                                                      運營的季節性
   The Group is a supplier of fuel cell systems in fuel cell vehicle industry.    本集團是燃料電池汽車行業的燃料電池系
   Higher revenues and operating profits are usually expected in the              統供應商。通常預計下半年，尤其是第四
   second half of the year, especially in the fourth quarter of the year. The     季度會有更高的收入和營業利潤。上半年
   first half of the year was mainly focused on the R&D phase, and after the      主要集中在研發階段，公告（強制檢查）完
   announcement (compulsory inspection) is completed, production and              成後，生產和銷售將集中在下半年。
   sales will be concentrated in the second half of the year.

10. INCOME TAX                                                                10. 所得稅
  The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profits arising      本集團須以實體為單位就在本集團成員公
  in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members of the Group are        司住所及經營所在的司法管轄區產生或來
  domiciled and operate.                                                        自相關司法管轄區的利潤繳納所得稅。


  Chinese Mainland                                                              中國內地
  The subsidiaries incorporated in Chinese mainland are subject to              中國內地註冊成立的附屬公司須就根據
  tax at the statutory rate of 25% on the taxable profits determined in         2008年1月1日生效的《中華人民共和國企業
  accordance with the PRC Corporate Income Tax Law which became                 所得稅法》確定的應課稅利潤按25%的法定
  effective on 1 January 2008, except for those subject to tax preferential     稅率繳稅，但下文所載適用稅收優惠政策
  policy set out below:                                                         的附屬公司除外：


  REFIRE Technology, Jiangsu PANDO and Shanghai Unilia were granted             重塑科技、江蘇磐動及上海韻量獲授高新
  the qualification of High and New Technology Enterprises (“HNTE”).            技術企業（「高新技術企業」）資格。因此，
  Accordingly, the subsidiaries were entitled to a preferential corporate       這些附屬公司有權於年內享有15%的優惠企
  income tax rate of 15% during the year.                                       業所得稅稅率。


  Certain subsidiaries of the Group have applied the Small-Scaled Minimal       本集團的若干附屬公司已適用中國國家稅
  Profit Corporate Income Tax Preferential Policy announced by the PRC’s        務總局公佈的小型微利企業所得稅優惠政
  State Administration of Taxation. For small-scale low-profit enterprises,     策。就小型微利企業而言，減按25%計算應
  the policy of calculating the taxable income at a reduced rate of 25%         納稅所得額及按20%的稅率徵收企業所得稅
  and levying corporate income tax at a rate of 20% will continue to be in      的政策將持續生效至2027年12月31日。
  effect until 31 December 2027.


  Hong Kong                                                                     中國香港
  The subsidiary incorporated in Hong Kong is subject to Hong Kong              就於年內在香港產生的估計應課稅利潤而
  profits tax at the rate of 8.25% for taxable income not exceeding             言，在香港註冊成立的附屬公司須就不超
  HKD2,000,000, and 16.5% for taxable income exceeding HKD2,000,000 on          過2 百萬港元的應評稅利潤按8.25%及就應
  any estimated assessable profits arising in Hong Kong during the year.        評稅利潤中超過2 百萬港元的部分按16.5%
  No provision for Hong Kong profits tax has been made as the Group had         的稅率繳納香港利得稅。由於本集團於年
  no assessable profits derived from or earned in Hong Kong during the          內並無源自香港或在香港賺取的應課稅利
  year.                                                                         潤，故並無作出香港利得稅撥備。


  Canada                                                                        加拿大
  The subsidiary incorporated in Canada is subject to Canada profits tax        在加拿大註冊成立的附屬公司須就於年內
  at the statutory rate of 15% on any estimated assessable profits arising      在加拿大產生的估計應課稅利潤按15%的法
  in Canada during the year. No provision for Canada profits tax has been       定稅率繳納加拿大利得稅。由於本集團於
  made as the Group had no assessable profits derived from or earned in         年內並無源自加拿大或在加拿大賺取的應
  Canada during the year.                                                       課稅利潤，故並無作出加拿大利得稅撥備。

   of tax prevailing in the jurisdictions in which the Group operates.            團經營所在司法管轄區的現行稅率計算。

14. LEASES                                                                   14. 租賃
   The Group as a lessee                                                       本集團作為承租人
   The Group has lease contracts for various items of plant and properties     本集團就其經營中使用的多項廠房及物業
   used in its operations. Leases of plant and properties generally have       項目訂有租賃合同。廠房及物業的租賃的
   lease terms between 2 and 8 years. Other equipment generally has lease      租期通常為2 年至8 年。其他設備的租期通
   terms of 12 months or less or is individually of low value. Generally,      常不超過 12 個月或個別而言價值較低。本
   the Group is restricted from assigning and subleasing the leased assets     集團一般被限制將租賃資產轉讓及轉租到
   outside the Group.                                                          本集團之外。

21. T R A D E A N D B I L L S R E C E I V A B L E S A N D                     21. 貿易應收款項及應收票據以及
    CONTRACT ASSETS (continued)                                                   合約資產（續）
   (a) Non-current trade receivables represented receivables                   (a) 非 流 動 貿 易 應 收 款 項 為 應 收 信
       from customers who signed contracts with a credit                           用期超過 1 年的已訂約客戶的款
       period of more than 1 year. (continued)                                     項。（續）
       The Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs             本集團在計算貿易應收款項、商業承
       for trade receivables, commercial acceptance bills and contract           兌匯票及合約資產的預期信貸虧損時
       assets. Trade receivable, commercial acceptance bills and contract        採用簡化方法。與其他客戶無類似信
       assets relating to customers not sharing similar credit risk with         用風險的客戶（如存在已知財務困難或
       others are assessed individually for impairment allowance, for            收回款項重大存疑的客戶）有關的貿易
       instance, customers with known financial difficulties or significant      應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約資產
       doubt on collection. The remaining trade receivables, commercial          會進行單項評估減值撥備。其餘貿易
       acceptance bills and contract assets are grouped and collectively         應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約資產
       assessed for impairment allowance. Under the collective approach,         予以分組並進行組合評估減值撥備。
       an impairment analysis is performed at each reporting date                根據組合法，於各報告日期採用撥備
       using a provision matrix to measure expected credit losses. The           矩陣進行減值分析，以計量預期信貸
       provision rates are based on past due information for grouping of         虧損。撥備率乃基於具有類似損失模
       customers that have similar loss patterns. The calculation reflects       式的客戶組別的逾期資料計算。該計
       the probability-weighted outcome, the time value of money and             算反映概率加權結果、貨幣時間值及
       reasonable and supportable information that is available at the           於報告日期可得的有關過往事件、當
       reporting date about past events, current conditions and forecasts        前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的合理有
       of future economic conditions. Generally, trade receivables are           理據資料。一般而言，貿易應收款項
       written off if past due for more than five years and are not subject      若逾期超過五年則予以撇銷且不受強
       to enforcement activity. During the year ended 31 December 2024           制執行活動所規限。截至2024 年及
       and 2025, the increase in impairment losses on trade receivables,         2025 年12 月31 日止年度，貿易應收款
       commercial acceptance bills receivable and contract assets was            項、應收商業承兌匯票及合約資產減
       mainly due to the rise in the expected loss provision matrix along        值損失增加主要是由於預期虧損撥備
       with the ageing migration.                                                矩陣隨著賬齡遷徙而上升。


       Set out below is the information about the credit risk exposure on        下表載列使用撥備矩陣計算的本集團
       the Group’s trade receivables, commercial acceptance bills and            貿易應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約
       contract assets using a provision matrix:                                 資產面臨的信用風險資料：

   a market-based valuation technique based on assumptions that are not          市 場 的 估 值 技 術（基 於 並 非 以 可 觀 察 市 價
   supported by observable market prices or rates. The valuation requires        或費率作為支撐的假設）而進行估算。估值
   the directors to determine comparable public companies (peers) based          要求董事根據行業、規模、槓桿作用及策
   on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate     略釐定可比較公眾公司（同業），並就每一
   price multiple, such as price to book (“P/B”) multiple and price to sales     識別的可比較公司計算適當的價格倍數，
   (“P/S”) multiple for each comparable company identified. The multiple is      如市賬率（「市賬率」）倍數及市銷率（「市銷
   calculated by dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company         率」）倍數。該倍數是按可比較公司的企業
   by an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then discounted               價值除以盈利計量值計算。交易倍數其後
   for considerations such as illiquidity and size differences between           則根據公司特定事實及情況就可比較公司
   the comparable companies based on company specific facts and                  之間的非流動性及規模差異等考慮因素予
   circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the corresponding        以折現。經折現倍數應用於非上市股權投
   earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments to measure the            資的相應盈利計量以計量公允價值。董事
   fair value. The directors believe that the estimated fair values resulting    認為，記錄在綜合財務狀況表的由估值技
   from the valuation technique, which are recorded in the consolidated          術產生的估算公允價值和記錄在其他全面
   statement of financial position, and the related changes in fair values,      收益的公允價值相關變動屬合理，並認為
   which are recorded in other comprehensive income, are reasonable, and         該等項目為在報告期末最為適當的價值。
   that they were the most appropriate values at the end of the reporting
   period.

    AND POLICIES
   The Group’s principal financial instruments, comprise bank loans, other           本集團的主要金融工具包括銀行貸款、其
   interest-bearing borrowings, and cash. The main purpose of these                  他計息借款以及現金。這些金融工具的主
   financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s operations. The         要目的是為本集團的經營籌集資金。本集
   Group has various other financial assets and liabilities such as trade and        團擁有多項其他金融資產及負債，如經營
   bills receivables and trade and bills payables, which arise directly from its     中直接產生的貿易應收款項及應收票據以
   operations.                                                                       及貿易應付款項及應付票據。


   The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are interest        本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為利率風
   rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of    險、外匯風險、信用風險及流動性風險。
   directors reviews and agrees policies for managing each of these risks            董事會審閱及協定管理這些風險中每項風
   and they are summarised below.                                                    險的政策，有關風險概述於下文。


   Interest rate risk                                                                利率風險
   The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in market interest rates              本集團面臨的市場利率變動風險主要與本
   relates primarily to the Group’s long term debt obligations with a                集團以浮動利率計息的長期債務責任有
   floating interest rate. The Group’s policy is to manage its interest cost         關。本集團的政策是採用固定及可變利率
   using a mix of fixed and variable rate debts.                                     債務組合管理其利息成本。


   As at 31 December 2025, if the interest rate of bank and other                    於2025 年12 月31 日，倘銀行及其他借款的
   borrowings had increased/decreased by 50 basis points and all other               利率上升╱下跌 50 個基點且所有其他變量
   variables were held constant, the loss before tax of the Group, through           保持不變，本集團的除稅前虧損通過對浮
   the impact on floating rate borrowings, would have increased/decreased            動利率借款的影響將增加╱減少約人民幣
   by approximately RMB6,159,000 (2024: RMB3,776,000).                               6,159,000元（2024年：人民幣3,776,000元）。

  procedures.                                                                     況下，本集團不會提供信用條款。

45. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                         45. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）
    AND POLICIES (continued)
   Liquidity risk                                                                 流動性風險
   The Group monitors and maintains a level of cash and cash equivalents          本集團監控現金及現金等價物並將其維持
   deemed adequate by the management of the Group to finance the                  在本集團管理層認為充足的水平，以為經
   operations and mitigate the effects of fluctuations of cash flows.             營撥資並削弱現金流量波動的影響。


   The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance between continuity of           本集團的目標是通過採用計息銀行及其他
   funding and flexibility through the use of interest-bearing bank and           借款以及租賃負債在資金持續性與靈活性
   other borrowings and lease liabilities.                                        之間維持平衡。


   Due to the Group’s supplier finance arrangements, the relevant trade           由於本集團的供應商融資安排，相關貿易
   payables are due to a single counterparty rather than individual               應付款項為應付單一交易對手方而非個別
   suppliers. This results in the Group being required to settle a significant    供應商的款項。這導致本集團須與單一交
   amount with a single counterparty, rather than less significant amounts        易對手方結算一大筆款項，而非與數名供
   with a number of suppliers. However, the Group’s payment terms for             應商結算金額較少的款項。然而，該等安
   trade payables covered by the arrangements are either identical to             排涵蓋的本集團貿易應付款項的付款期與
   the payment terms for other trade payables or extended to not more             其他貿易應付款項的付款期相同或延長至
   than 180 days. Management does not consider the supplier finance               不超過180天。鑒於付款期未大幅延長，管
   arrangements to result in excessive concentrations of liquidity risk           理層認為供應商融資安排不會導致流動性
   given the payment terms are not significantly extended. Details of the         風險過度集中。該等安排的詳情披露於財
   arrangements are disclosed in note 25 to the financial statements.             務報表附註25。


   The maturity profile of the Group’s financial liabilities as at the end of     本集團於報告期末基於已訂合約未折現付
   the reporting period, based on the contractual undiscounted payments,          款的金融負債的到期情況如下：

45. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                       45. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）
    AND POLICIES (continued)
  Capital management                                                            資本管理
  The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to               本集團資本管理的主要目標是保障本集團
  safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and to           持續經營並維持良好資本比率的能力，以
  maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business and          支持其業務並最大化股東價值。
  maximise shareholders’ value.


  The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments to it           本集團管理其資本結構，並根據經濟狀況
  in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or adjust the         的變化對其進行調整。為維持或調整資本
  capital structure, the Group may return capital to shareholders or issue      結構，本集團或會退還股東資本或發行新
  new shares.                                                                   股。


  The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net debt           本 集 團 採 用 資 產 負 債 比 率（按 債 務 淨 額 除
  divided by total capital plus net debt. The Group includes, within            以總資本再加上債務淨額計算）監控資本。
  net debt, interest-bearing bank and other borrowings, trade and bills         本集團的債務淨額包括計息銀行及其他借
  payables, other payables and accruals and lease liabilities, less cash and    款、貿易應付款項及應付票據、其他應付
  cash equivalents. Capital includes equity attributable to the owners of       款項及應計費用及租賃負債，減現金及現
  the parent.                                                                   金等價物。資本包括母公司擁有人應佔權
                                                                                益。


  The Group has established supplier finance arrangements to manage its         本集團已設立供應商融資安排以管理其營
  working capital, details of which are included in note 25 to the financial    運資金，其詳情載於財務報表附註25。
  statements.
